there are a few threads tugging my mind rn. but i guess we can start with... the obvious, the mundane. im sitting at sightglass - in the mission. they were able to open it back up this am after yest close down due to the flood. and things are back and running! but yea app. they had to do a lot of work to clean up the mess and whatnot, but seems good now. <!-- beat:891 -->

sf's inability to prepare for and manage the flooding yest reminded me of the flimsiness of human creation when confronted with nature itself. and nature is great at reminding us on a regular basis that we aren't all that we think we are. the seat of the greatest technological, economic advancement humanity has ever witnessed can't handle a bit of rain. lol. <!-- beat:892 -->

ok. let's open up to the present moment. i'm listening to angel - massive attack. a classic. a song i've really liked. i think i first discovered it through house? the tv show. maybe their opening wasn't angel, but it was by the same group which exposed me to this kind of music. really cool. <!-- beat:893 -->

i'm sipping on my americano with a teeny bit of half and half to give it a little bit of thickness and richness. as i'm typing away at my magic keyboard resting on this desk with my phone above it restig on the airpods case to give it a bit of an angle so that i can see the text that i'm typing. and yea, that's about it. that's my scene right now. <!-- beat:894 -->

ok. mind is not feeling as smooth rn. feels a bit "rough". things are not flowing as they did during the in the zone phases of yesterday. but that is expected! the mind is constantly changing. it's important to not be attached to any particular state of mind because it will not last. change is the only constant. and not just linear change - the kind you can predict and adapt to - but nonlinear, accelearting change is the norm. the kind that you cannot intellectually prepare for, that you can only surrender to. choas of sorts. chaos that follows a pattern. <!-- beat:895 -->

ok. well, the mvp / the streaming experience feels vv clear. i woke up this morning imagining some of its interactions. i think i dreamed about it. i woke up "feeling" the interaction from streaming, π showing me a chat block, me pressing enter at the end of my para and the input in the chat block takes focus, and i press enter again without typing in that chat box and a new para starts below it. oh, and a cool thing that happened then in my mind is that the chat block collapsed into a line with "chat" in the center of the line - like a divider - so that you know a chat happened there, but don't know what was chatted. this is i think a good way to do this because leaving the chat as is on screen can be distracting. once i've moved my attention past the chat block, the chat block should sort of fade away - but not completely because technically speaking it still "exists" in the stream! <!-- beat:896 -->

this means that every block has a focus state and a non-focus state - and it animates between these states when it receives and loses focus. that is important. and this is a diff kind of focus than the input focus. the block itself is visually different between its focused and unfocused states. like the chat block in unfocused does not even render the input text field. great. this is good. and the goal once again is to make it easy / seamless for my attention to go where i want it to go without things getting in its way. that's the core principle here. and this principle will manifest in many ways throughout this streaming ux / experience. <!-- beat:897 -->

ok. yea. dreaming. the subconscious mind working. that is powerful. many people think spending 12 hours on somethign (like the entire day) is like only twice as impactful as spending 6 hours on it. that's so far from the truth. when you become obsessed about something you activate your subconscious. you start to dream about it. different parts of your mind start to work on it. spending all of your waking hours on something is far far - by many orders - more impactful than spending like only 6 hours or so on it. <!-- beat:898 -->

siddhartha talked about this as well. "na rincati" - without stopping, continuous. that is the key to success on the path. if you are carving out an hour or a few hours of your day to "practice" you are still approaching it in a fragmented way. every moment of existence is part of the practice. <!-- beat:899 -->

had a great convo with parents the other day - well we chatted yest as well, but i think this particular convo was from the day before. so my parents take this season - "karthika maasam" seriously. traditionally, this season is meant to be dedicated to spiritual pursuits. it's supposed to be more conducive to it or whatever. i have no idea. and if you'd asked siddhartha i think he'd call bullshit on it. but whatever this is part of the belief system of some hindus. anyway, my dad was talking about how he needs to move past schedules. he was like schedules pull you out of the moment. planning to meditate at 3am means that you're thinking about meditating at 3am at 2:30am rolling around in bed. the point is to move beyond schedules. if you are conscious, you must practice. if you are not conscious (sleeping), then whatever happens naturally happens. the best practice is no practice. the notion of "practicing" only exists if the counterpart of "not practicing" exists. if you eliminate not practicing, then you also eliminate practicing. and it becomes one smooth continuous flow. na rincati. <!-- beat:900 -->

that's kind of what i want with streaming! or rather expressing. or whatever the hell this thing is that i'm doing. i feel like life is simple. the entire process is just to open up to the truth of the present moment. to open myself up to, to surrender to, the natural movement of my mind right now. the subtler movements, not the coarse urges – and there is a v big diff between the two that i think i touched on earlier. <!-- beat:901 -->

i want streaming - expressing, learning, chatting with π to bring ideas to life, or whatever to happen as part of one continuous uninterrupted stream of consciousness. just moving from moment to moment naturally. without stopping anywhere. <!-- beat:902 -->

on the notion of not stopping, takuan soho comes to mind. he was a zen master from the time of miyamoto musashi - perhaps the greatest swordsman to have ever existed. it's not clear if takuan and musashi actually crossed paths in reality, but in the book "musashi" by eiji yoshikawa, they share a great friendship and takuan sort of plays the mentor role in the relationship. i think in the story takuan is like a decade or so older than musashi, and also very wise and in tune with The Way. <!-- beat:903 -->

takuan wrote this piece, this essay of sorts, called the unfettered mind to yagyu munenori i think? another famed swordsman of his time. and in that essay takuan talks about the notion of a mind that does not stop. let's say you are in the midst of a battle. you have a katana in your hands and so does your opponent standing in front of you. it is a life and death situation. one wrong move and you will cease to exist. this is the situation of the moment. what do you focus on? if you focus on the sword of the opponent, you miss their footwork, their movement that will lead to you getting slashed. if you focus on their feet, you miss the sword's movemetn. if you focus on anything – if your mind "stops" anywhere – you will be killed. so what you do? you do not let your mind rest anywhere. this does not mean you keep moving your mind everywhere intentionally, this does not mean you keep moving your eyes around in some pattern to be able to perceive everything. you settle into natural awareness. you settle into a balance of the mind. into the natural movement of hte mind. you surrender to the instinct. your instinct will move your attention where it needs to go, that is not even your job. you are just present. read to act / react based on whatever the situation presents. you do not force. you do not premeditate. you do not stop your mind anywhere. this is the mind that does not stop anywhere. a continuous natural mind. ready for anything and everything, because it is nothing. <!-- beat:904 -->

this is the mind that is important for anything. whether you are playing basketball, building a company or whatever. the moment your mind stops somewhere, you have started to dig your own grave. google's mind stopped at search. they drank their own kool-aid about how awesome search is and whatnot. and now they've been slashed straight through their torso, and are now a walking corpse that is going to fall. do not let your mind stop. <!-- beat:905 -->

feeling proud for doing something good is your mind stopping. becoming anxious or sad because you did something off - bec you made a mistake - is your mind stopping. basketball taught me a lot of things, and i think one of the most important things it taught was to stay in the moment. if you miss a shot and your mind lingers on that miss for even a split second, you will not get back on defense in time and give up a fast break. if you look at the greats - take steph curry - when he misses, you can see that his mind has completely forgotten about it. his body is already shifting into defense and getting ready to react to what the other team is doing. he's great at this. steph is like a zen monk on the court. he is in tune with the moment. his mind does not stop (except in rare occasions, but hey we're all human after all). <!-- beat:906 -->

anyway, where am i? a mind that does not stop. that's what it feels like right now. this stream is flowing out of my mind through my hands. and my mind isn't stopping. it's moving. i don't really know the next words that will come out of my mind and through my hands and onto this screen in front of me. and that's a good thing. it means i'm not premeditating. it means that my mind isn't stopping anywehre - whether the future or the past or anything else - it's just flowing. this is the way to stream.  <!-- beat:907 -->

that being said, as i've talked about earlier, it is entirely possible to have judgement about what to express, what trains of thought to take and what to let go of, to be mindful of this process. that is not the same as the mind stopping. and this feels like a paradox. but you have to experience it to understand that operating mindfully with good judgement can occur while the mind moves naturally, continuously, without stopping. and that paradox is perhaps where the greatest skill lies. <!-- beat:908 -->

ok. i was just blowing hot air into my hands bec it's chilly here. sf has gotten chilly. i like it tbh. it reminds me of my days in uttarakhand. in the mountains. i spent this season in uttarakhand back in 2024. and i also spent a month in april in 2025 there. high elevation. freezing basically. the wind pierces the skin. but it's beautiful. the cold forces you into the present moment. if you're someone who has difficulty remaining in the moment - if your mind wanders a lot - expose yourself to nature more. get out of your temperature regulated cozy home and into the rain, into the cold. get rid of your fat cushioned shoes and put on barefoot shoes so that you can feel every crack in the pavement that you walk in. don't insulate yourself from reality, and reality will naturally pull your mind into the present moment. take a cold shower in the cold weather and i promise you that your mind will not be able to wander much. well it will as you get used to the cold, but at least for some period your mind has no choice but to work with the reality of the moment because it is too jarring to ignore. and i think this is a good thing. this is the way i think we are meant to be. not insulating ourselves from reality, from nature, but embracing it. embrace the discomfort. find the ease in the discomfort and then a deeper sort of confidence emerges. and it's beautiful. it's more robust because it shows you that if you can deal with reality, then no shit you can deal with all the bullshit imaginations and delusions that run our world. <!-- beat:909 -->

that's the other thing. reality vs. fiction. comprehending and being continuously mindful of this distinction is important. else you will get lose in the fiction. i often go through the thought exercise: what happens if i fail? will i not have enough food to eat? no. will i not have a roof over my head and have to sleep on the street? no. the reality of my situation is really good. now if i look at, say, my bank balance, or if i say, think about how i may not be able to buy a house in tokyo and spend a year there doing something and feel bad about it - then i'm an idiot. i'm not using my mind in a skillful way. i am lost in fiction. my reality is great. no matter what happens in the world of fiction (whether my ideas work out or not, whether my personal stock valuation soars or falls, etc.) – in reality i am good. i can go on a walk through nature and experience it fully and become completely grounded in its truth and let go of fictions and find joy in that. lasting joy is found in reality, not in fictions. <!-- beat:910 -->

in my travels i've met some really cool people. there's this old man in the mountains in uttarakhand who i think is the closest person i've met to an enlightened, liberated being. he has this childlike joy and curiosity, and can, at a moment's notice, switch modes into whatever the situation needs in the moment he is always balanced. full of love. even when he scolds you. and i've gotten my fair share of scolding from him. haha. i love him. he's like 87 years old but in the course of a single conversation can feel like he's 3 years old, 30, or whatever. he wields his energy like a master. he reminds me of bhumi and uncle iroh from the avatar the last airbender. more bhumi i'd say - bec he has that playful mischevious side to him. and like both of them when the situation needs them to be serious, they get really serious (the white lotus stuff - iirc - having to fight the war) - this man reacts similarly. a few months ago i spent time at his ashram for like 10 days, and he discovered that someone who was working for him was a thief, and he dealt with that situation perfectly. his scream shook the entire building. and i was waiting right outside the room because i needed to talk to him about something. but yea he was ferocious as he needed to be to deal with the situation well. and yet he had love in his heart. when i interacted with him, there was no trace of his anger. he wielded anger as a tool to deal with the situation. he did not let anger wield him. this is. mastery. this is the kind of mind i want to train. this man has nothing to his name. everything that came around him was built by, donated by, others. he just does his thing. if the world wants more of him, the world just supports him with more money, people or whatever. if the world doesn't want him i think he's still be blissed out experiencing the reality within. i mean, look at that kind of mind. look at that person. is there anything more valuable that you can train? meeting him and reading about many of the greats throughout history is what has reinforced my unshakeable belief in the human potential. no ai can hold a candle up to people like this. their minds are untraceable. immeasurable. you cannot compare their minds on any axes / framework with ai. incomparable. they're operating at a different dimension. outside of all dimensions. i mean, it's incredible. you really have to witness it, spend time with such people to understand what they are capable of. and there are many such people. not sure how many are left today, probably very very few, but they have walked this earth. that's what i feel when i read about ajahn chah as well. people who have this presence about them. unshakeable minds. unfettered. i mean it's so clear to me that what they have achieved and unlocked is far far greater than whatever you can achieve materially. this old man in the mountains i was talking about has all sorts of people visiting him every day. every evening all the kids from the neighboring village and their families come over to sing bhajans and songs. the energy in the room is electrifying. and our old man is in a sort of meditative trance playing his harmonium(? - that piano keyboard with the air bag thing you have to press to make the sound). and then he teases and guides the kids who absolutely adore him. he's like their grandpa. he's like my grandpa. so much love in his heart. it's like unending. an ocean of love seemingly within a mere human body. beyond the laws of physics or whatever. anyway, people like this give me faith in humanity. they give me faith in the depth of human potential. they give me faith in myself. because i believe that if i work hard and train myself that i too can tap into that way of being. i think it's possible. and i actually think it's aligned with maximizing the value i create. these are not at odds. or maybe i'm deluded. lol. idk. but my goal is clear, maximizing the value i create, maximizing my personal stock valuation, this isn't something i'm going to budge from. onward. <!-- beat:911 -->

ok. i'm going to get another americano. a decaf one. because i need to save some caffeine reserves for a capuccino later. actually fuck it, let's just do that now and we'll do a decaf later. lool. no "saving space" or any of that. just move forward. focus on what you want right now in this moment unapologetically, and if it isn't against the way, go do it. it's that simple. <!-- beat:912 -->

R just made a reallly cute capp art. (π - remind me to paste video here). incredible haha. it's the little things like that make things special. no matter how good robots get at making coffee, imma come get it from a person to feel the warmth of their presence and artistry and for that exchange. that is what i want. <!-- beat:913 -->

oh D is back at the cafe - the guy i talked about a few days ago, 2 daughters who are doing well, etc. he's reading his kindle. nice. will say hi when he's a bit less in the zone. ok just did. nice to be able to see people and say hi and whatnot and just check in. there's a feeling of community. not necessarily a strong community or whatever, but that's the thing, little exchanges and interactions like this are actually beautiful. they make me feel like i'm a part of something larger - a part of the human spirit that expresses itself through many people, many generations, across many thousands of years. part of that essence. it's a nice feeling. i can't imagine living alone and isolated from people. well maybe for spurts - but even then i would wnat to be connected with other life - trees, animals, etc. living in like some. high rise manmade jail disconnected from nature and life is hell on earth. can't believe so many rich people live that way. it's kind of silly. goes back to the convo from a few days ago with F about how it's so easy to lose sight of the purpose of money and just earn it for the sake of earning it. that only leads to misery. money is fuel, but if you don't have a direction worth driving in, excess fuel starts to leak out of the tank and becomes dangerous. a single spark and you're lit on fire. <!-- beat:914 -->

ok all of these interactions. oh btw, D just hooked me up with a free cap. lol. it's like a running joke b/w us now. i don't want free coffee, i hate getting things like that for free, but i think they know that i hate it and so do it whenever they can. lol. i appreciate it don't get me wrong, but idk something about i don't want to take more than i give back? getting things for free feelsl ike presure someitmes. idk. but yea, little interactions. ran into probably 5 peeople here in this cafe over the past hour or so that i had a chat with. nothing deep. oh yea the lawyer L just got an offer from another law firm and was really excited about that. i congratulated him and we shared in that joy. that was nice. the piont is, littel interactions like this are awesome. it reminds me of some stories of like old school india - like the malgudi days etc. people are meant to be with other people. we are meant to have these kinds of interactions throughout the day, throuhgout our lives. they ground us. they are opportunities to share an experience with others. no matter how small those experiences may seem, they are still important. they feel good. they are natural. staying cooped up at home and "grinding" on something on your laptop or whatever is unnatural af. <!-- beat:915 -->

on this note, i just realized that i def want to stream from my phone. i don't want to have to do this on a laptop. i don't want to lug around a laptop. i don't want to pull it out. i just want to stream and do everyhting from my phone! that's the goal. i was iniitally thinking that the mvp would be on my laptop only, but fuck that. let's make this a mobile first experience. why not? why not? we have intelligence on tap brother, why the fuck not? build what you need. stop confining yourself to some narrow notion of an mvp. be expansive. think abundantly. <!-- beat:916 -->

alright. yea, mobile first. i want to keep walking through sf - from cafe to cafe to park to park - streaming. that's really all i want to do. meet people along the way. learn. follow my curiosity. express myself. i mean this is the life. and if i run out of money? then i hop on a plane and wander through the mountains of india - hut to hut, village to village, and keep streaming. this feels so natural. meet people. express. build. create. live. die. i would not regret this life at all if i remain on this path without straying, without getting caught up in dumb ways of living, without getting lost in fictions. <!-- beat:917 -->

ok. mobile first is good. let's operate with that as the assumption. and it really lends itself well to like making videos capturing visuals, etc. i want to do more of that! like take more videos of what i'm seeing and experiencing. that is nice. v cool. <!-- beat:918 -->

ok, coming back to... NO! no "coming back" haha. that is the wrong question. where am i? what exists in this present moment that i need to open myself up to. get out of the fictions and lean into the reality of this moment. lean into the feeling of this moment. stay true to it. <!-- beat:919 -->

the one problem with mobile first is that iteration speed is ass. bec of hte whole apple distribution bullshit. how can we get around this? is there any way to sort of jailbreak or whatever this phone and just get full control over it and drop ai in there and just prompt it to build the stream and whatnot? like i don't want to have to go to my computer every time i want to update the app / streaming thing. everything needs to be able to happen right here. but i think that's tough. or rather, that gets into a reserach that feels unnecessary rn. let me go chat with codex, will see if there's a way here. <!-- beat:920 -->

alright, it's figuring it out. i'm back in the stream. see that context switch? that's bullshit. that would be eliminated entirely once the stream is set up. uugh, why can't i just have the app rn.  <!-- beat:921 -->

ok. what is the approach here? kind of messy eh. i wish someone else could just build this for me. i'm so lazy. i don't want to do anything that is not on my natural path. i don't want to do anything that is not the natural movement of my mind. i don't want to pull or push or suppress or oppress my natural movement. i need ai to get way way better. fast. <!-- beat:922 -->

actually, what about this: an app that renders itself according to the code within the app itself. and then π can just edit the code within the app from the app itself and update it? i mean that's still prob going to run into a ton of roadblocks - spinning up processes and whatnot, well that can happen on the laptop remotely. <!-- beat:923 -->

[https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=k2Gw2fdXWSs&si=LOXEcN0uomwwSO4o&drli=1](https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=k2Gw2fdXWSs&si=LOXEcN0uomwwSO4o&drli=1) - banger. wow. feels like the background music for someone seeking truht and about to discover somethign profound lol. use it for a short film. <!-- beat:924 -->

alright. looks like this is going to take some work to set up streaming from my phone. but yea today is the day to get this off the ground. i don't want to stream from notes and stuff anymore. it's time to get the right set up so that the compounding can start. <!-- beat:925 -->

i was initially considering walking back home from sightglass on 20th, but decided to walk out to sightglass on divis instead. and i think the reason for why is important - there is an underlying principle here that applies to i think every time i prioritize. so what is it? <!-- beat:926 -->

firstly, i initially wanted to go home bec i wanted to go and implement the product - which i need my laptop to do. so i thought it naturally made sense to go back and bring it to life and continue streaming from the laptop. but that's a blunder. why? because my mind is really fresh rn and executing the idea actually requires less freshness. that's the key sort of principle: do more difficult cognitive work the fresher the mind is, and less cognitively intense work the more tired the mind is. <!-- beat:927 -->

working with π to bring the product to life and iterate on it is vastly easier work than figuring out what to create in the first place. vastly easier than understnading the right featureset, scope, etc. if we look at this stream that led to the mvp design - that is the tough work. to explore and understand my desires, objectives, etc. once you have the scope in place and can imagine what you want, bringing that to life is easy peasy. in fact, in a few months or years, π would have already brought it to life given what i've streamed till now! it's not there yet (or maybe it is, and i just don't know bec. this isn't wired up yet), but the point is that the tough work happens here in the stream, not in the execution of the vision – which will increasingly be done entirely by π / ai. <!-- beat:928 -->

ok, so my mind is fresh. rather than waste it executing on an idea i can already see in my mind, let's explore what's next. so in a few hours i will go home and implement the stream. imagine we already have it. imagine this is that experience? what comes next? <!-- beat:929 -->

i want to think through layers of expression. oh, and before that (well who knows if / when we'll get back to that) let me talk about streaming vs. tweeting vs. essays. i have a few unfinished essays that i'm not sure i'll ever go back to! that's the thing. it is vv rare for me to want to go back to an essay. why is that? why does it feel unnatural to go back and finish it? well i think the answer is in the prev sentence. going "back" does not feel right. i want to move forward. if i've uncovered an insight / something - then i want to build on top of it, not polish it for publishing or whatever. and yet publishing an idea is really important because that's how i can get feedback from others with far better ideas for how to improve it than i can have. so there is a tension here. let's explore that tension. <!-- beat:930 -->

on one hand i just want to stream to understand something, and once i have understood it i just want to move on. and yet in the long-run this is suboptimal af because it restricts getting feedback from others! so i absolutely want to publish, but i don't want to spend much time polishing and publishing... so what to do? i mean, my current list of unfinished essays are evidence of my disinterest in going "back" and polishing and publishing something. what to do? <!-- beat:931 -->

i think there's a nice middle ground / "middle path" approach here. i think for every conclusion i arrive at after thinking that is worth sharing, i should share it right then and there. not as a polished essay - bec who knows if / when i will ever get to that - but as a simple, concise tweet that encapsulates the idea. and i would tweet it and below the tweet have a link to the location in the stream that has that point. why? because the paras / surrounding text likely has context for why i believe that - which if someone was interested can read / run through their ai / ask π (eventually) about it. so that essentially achieves the objective: have a crisp concise summary / essence of the idea, with a way for others to see the line of reasoning (through ai - bec. who would wade through this clutter and make sense of it). this feels enough. and if i really really from the bottom of my heart feel like it's time to craft a polished artifact and put it out into the world in a more meaningful way, then i can do that! but waiting for that should not prevent me from sharing ideas through the stream - to basically create opportunities for luck - for others to vibe with it and give me feedback and improve my thinking. <!-- beat:932 -->

so yea, and i think there's a pattern i can follow. like right now the preceding para has a nugget of something that i may want to post. and now i would think about what that is in one sentence, and craft it right here in the stream. and once it's there, i would tell π to publish it. that simple. <!-- beat:933 -->

so what would that one liner be? actually, this also feels like "going back", so i think the better approach here is to just instruct π to find the tweet in the stuff above and propose it! and we can have a skill for this - and eventually train a model for this specific action. this feels like the way to go. i want to keep moving forward unincumbered. <!-- beat:934 -->

(π - find the tweet above) - something to this effect. <!-- beat:935 -->

ok, good. and i think this would be the workflow moving forward. i have a feeling this will lead to a lot of tweeting. maybe more than is good? idk, but we will figure that out in motion, that isn't something to think about rn. <!-- beat:936 -->

there was another thought that came to mind during my walk. oh yea, walking convos. i would love to have a little robot on wheels in front of me recording me, have a mic on me, and a few more mics in my pocket, share my location publicly - as i walk through the city - and basically create an opportunity for anyone to walk up to me and have a chat while we walk that is recorded, perhaps edited by π after the fact and shared with everyone for their approval before shipping it. so basically a way to capture and ship these sorts of walking conversations. i think this would be really fun and cool. and it creates a surface for serendipity. <!-- beat:937 -->

that's the thing. you have to position yourself to get lucky. if you sit at home and just keep ideas to yourself and stay to yourself, you create no room for luck to operate. the right people aren't going to come knocking at your door excited to meet you and learn about what you're doing. you have to position yourself for lady luck to grace you with her presence and beauty. this is a principle for how to operate. being open. publishing streaming, walking through the city, starting a walking podcast and publishing conversations, etc. are all ways to basically create a pleasant space for lady luck to visit. and have the chai boiling on low and clean cups ready and maybe some warm soft cookies ready to greet her with. that's what i think is important to do. <!-- beat:938 -->

ok, good. that makes sense. where am i? oh yea, coming back to sharing the stream below the tweet. that leads to some questions about how this stream would be represented. each para here is basically like a "line". and each "line" or "beat" -- oooh i like the word "beat" here. like a heartbeat. a beat of my consciousness. each beat is like a para here in this stream, a block, a whatever. and each beat has its own unique identifier. and going to koopuluri.com/stream/29303924 or whatever should open up that specific beat that is highlighted on screen, while the visitor can scroll up or down. that would be cool. so even if π finds the tweet in the stream, proposes it and we post it - which becomes a beat much later - π would still post it with the beat from which the point actually arose, not the later beat when it posted it. right? yea. so there's that little thing to take care of - so basically π produces the tweet and asks for approval to publish, and also shows me which beat it's going to link to below the tweet that contains the thinking that led to the tweet. good. this is the workflow that i want. <!-- beat:939 -->

coming back to the energy. right now bec my mind feels fresh, all sorts of fun ideas come to the mind. about features i want, ux etc. later in the day / night my mind is more tired and doesn't naturally have ideas in this way. that is the time for execution. at least in these early days as i'm getting streaming set up. once straeming is set up well with π - π will be executing all the time! and i can just do what is natural to me. as ai gets better, the less i have to think about or worry about execution, and the more i can just follow the natural movement of my mind. i'm excited for that. <!-- beat:940 -->

but remember, excitement isn't a state i want to hold. excitement leads to delusion, leads to making mistakes becasue it is a sign of attachment. that's the thing. i'm not a fan of saying things like "i'm really excited to launch XYZ" or "i'm looking forward to ABC', etc. these are phrases that are really common in our every day use, but i actually don't want to feel that. i don't want to "look forward" to something, i just want to look at what is here right now. i'm a patient. very. i would much rather be absorbed in the present moment not looking forward, and experience that future moment only when it manifests in the present. similarly i don't want to be excited to launch anything. if i'm excited to launch something i will get skeptical of it. i will not trust my intuition. i want to launch things when my mind feels vv balanced, when i'm actually not excited to launch it, but am already thinking about what's next. that's the right time to "launch" something. <!-- beat:941 -->

cool. alright. what is in front of me right now? there are some really cool things that we can do with the stream. every beat has an identifier. a series of beats becomes a "train" of thought. a sequence of beats is a train. and that's it. or rather, even within a beat it can be a train. if i look above, some of these beats are long af. so i guess the terminology gets sorta muddled here. but that's ok! the terminology doesn't matter in the same way that a bird is a bird no matter whether you call it a bird or not. - this is a really important idea that i think i remember feynman talking about. and def something that jiddu krishanmurthi really emphasized. what does it mean to understand something? to understand the truth about something. the truth lies beyond labels. labels, words, etc. are merely pointers to the truth. when the wise point to something don't just stare at their finger, look at what they want you to see. <!-- beat:942 -->

sightglass on divis is really nice. i'm right near the entrance. like if you walk in and look a bit to the left, i'm sitting on that sofa thing with the cushion, that's angled so that i'm literally facing directly toward the entrance. and so i have a view of the cars and people walking on the street past the cafe. it's a nice little setting to stream. a really nice setting. :). in this moment, life is good. and the funny thing is, life only exists in this moment. so... life is good. <!-- beat:943 -->

back to the site stuff. in addition to koopuluri.com/stream/<beat_id>, we should be able to do <beat1_id>:<beat2_id> - and that would open up the stream with the entire range highlighted. that would be really cool. yes. and eventually this is how the training data would be compiled? every artifact i produce will correspond to a train of thought that led to it (including all the iteration with π!). and then we can use these trains - this flow of consciousness - coupled with the final output, coupled with its impact on my personal stock valuation to create the training data. <!-- beat:944 -->

ok. let's settle into the moment. where am i? i'm easing into the moment, at a beautiful cafe, in a beautiful city, on a beautiful day, surrounded by beautiful people. simple. this is the reality of my moment right now. i feel fresh. alert. listening to a great beat - [https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=ro4PId4p4Vk&si=8FdFKJDD9Me1YOc8&drli=1](https://music.youtube.com/watch?v=ro4PId4p4Vk&si=8FdFKJDD9Me1YOc8&drli=1) - discovered this artist (/group?) today. public memory. really nice vibe. i like their music. <!-- beat:945 -->

let's talk about the privacy model for the stream. this is really important to get right. the point is not to be fully public / transparent. that is too extreme. there is a middle path here that we have to find. with a bias toward more transparent - so that we can be a more enticing target for lady luck. <!-- beat:946 -->

ok. how to think about transparency? let's lean into this... how transparent do i want to be? a few beats above i talked about wanting to share my live location as i walk through the city with an open invitation for a walking conversation. that is something i want to do. that is pretty transparent. ok let me work backward. what do i want to keep private? well firstly, i want to have some space betweeen expression and publishing - even for the things i want to publish, just so that we have some space to check for and enforce right speech. i don't want to put negativity into the world. if i accidentaly say something negative about someone or something, i do not want that to be published. this doesn't mean i want to remove it from my stream. almost certainly not! i would like to retain the natural movement of my mind. dislikes can also be valuable training data for π. like maybe π surfaces a piece of content and i tell it i don't like it and to not show me things like that again – this is information that must never be published to the world. creating something, creating anything, is brutal. i know how tough it can be to take the risk to pursue creation and the sort of fear and anxiety it can induce to put something into the world. even if i don't like what someone puts out, i would not want to express my displeasure / dislike of their work to the artist themselves. ever. maybe there is an exception to this, but i genuinely cannot think of a single instance across my life when i think it was worthwhile to express a dislike of someones work to them. even if it's pure evil, i'd rather just put my attention toward creating work and shaping the world that fights against that evil, rather than tell the person directly. like if hitler was alive right now, i would certainly not write to him telling him that he is evil or whatever. i would focus on figuring out ways to stop him. whatever htat looks like. i guess there is nuance here. cerators can be evil. ofc. hitler "created" many things technically, but they caused immense suffering to many people. anyway, my point is, i do not want to publish negativity. and this is a core requirement of right speech. and yet i want to capture that information to train π. <!-- beat:947 -->

ok great, so there is a notion of filtering what i publish even though i may capture much more information. obviously. i do not want to be "extremely transparent". i want to be optimally transparent. ok what else do i want to avoid? <!-- beat:948 -->

direct messages with people. actually i think this is somewhat simple. my x DM will be the primary way the outside world can reach me. and my responses to my X DMs will be public information! they will just be a part of this stream! if a convo goes private - it moves to iMessage, and nothing, not even π, will have access to those conversations. either iMessage or whatsapp – complete privacy guaranteed. a simple boundary. one that also feels really easy to be mindful of. <!-- beat:949 -->

what else would i note want to publish? what about financial infromation? naturally i'm going to wire up my mercury to π so that it knows all of my transactions, balance, and can even buy stuff for me (with some limits - maybe a virtual card exclusively for π). <!-- beat:950 -->

i don't think this is a bad idea to publish? why do i feel that? well, what do i have to hide? see, the thing is, i want to share how expensive it is to train / use π to help me across everything. this entire streaming / living this way – this "way of life" – is an experiment and i want to be transparent about the costs to do so. i view this sort of like how i view bryan johnson and his blueprint exploraiton. being open about my costs will help me have more productive convos with others who want to try this out. and may even help others on their journeys to create using ai or whatever. <!-- beat:951 -->

and for transactions i want to keep private, i just use my personal card! my boa transactions are all private, my mercury transactions are all public. great. <!-- beat:952 -->

what else would i want private? my browsing history perhaps? that's a reasonable start. so when someone sees the stream, they don't actually see the browser blocks, maybe they just see the browser block with a one para / one line π summary of what i did during that session in a way that doesn't reveal the nitty gritty details. <!-- beat:953 -->

chats with π can be public? if there is sensitive information in the chat, then π can just hide that. it can throw in a REDACTED in it. <!-- beat:954 -->

that's a good sort of model. in the stream, anything wrapped in a <REDACTED>poop</REDACTED> (or whatever syntax makes sense) shows up in my data for myself and π, but is not public. this is reasonable. and when i see the stream myself, the portions that are redacted may just show up in red. great. that is a good ux. it makes it vv clear to me what is public and what isn't. if an entire block is redacted, its border would just be red or something. yea this feels p clear, there's some details to figure out, but i'm sure π can do it. <!-- beat:955 -->

what else? what about assets? those should be public unless they are redacted. same principle! if a video is in the stream, and it is redacted, its border would just be red. and to toggle any redactions etc. i just talk to π. the usual. everything happens through π. the entire system is starting to come together. feels really simple. <!-- beat:956 -->

and this is enough to get started. we can figure out and iterate on the privacy model in motion. <!-- beat:957 -->

what else? where is my mind rn? we have the stream. we have the privacy model. i now know what it looks / feels like when it is published. oh, what is the publishing flow? i talked about having some "space" between streaming and actually publishing it - the "space" where we can think about if there are things we want to redact, and be mindful about what we put out into the world. let's talk about that space. <!-- beat:958 -->

also what i just realized is that the mvp for the stream app doesn't even need to expose the underlying filesystem. if i ever want to view it, or open artifacts in it directly or whatever - i can do that in obsidian or cursor or whatever. i don't need to do that here! that's the point. and this is a great forcing funciton. knowing myself i'm going to get pissed (i mean annoyed lol) whenever i have to context switch like that, and that pain will force me to make the stream good enough wehre i can do everything in it. <!-- beat:959 -->

so the stream is where i do everything. and the underlying filesystem - look if i want to know what it has and whatnot, i can just explore by asking π right then and there. it's not that deep. but yea, the stream will increasingly be how i do everything. <!-- beat:960 -->

ok for the "publishing". i'm basically seeing a subtle bar on the left edge of the streaming pane that is green for the portion of the stream that is published, red if that beat contains a redaction, and gray if it is unpublished / unreviewed so far. so when i just glance. i can see how much has not been published yet. <!-- beat:961 -->

how do we publish? do i really want to chat with π about publishing? no. i want π to be responsible for it. after some number of unpublished beats, π will just review it and publish if it feels that there is nothing to be redacted. if there are redactions, π will show me what it wants to redact and why, and on approval it publishes. actually even this, there is no need to ask me. ACTUALLY; the better flow is - if i want something to be redacted during the steam i'll prob just mention in the beat itself? like there is nothing so far, that i'd want redacted. i don't think so at least. ok yea, i want the beats about me talking about that cute woman yest at sightglass to be redacted – and this instruction about what i want redacted. good. <!-- beat:962 -->

so i can instruct what i want redacted within the stream, and that is applied. or rather than auto publish, what if i just have the word "publish" on a new line? like: <!-- beat:963 -->

publsh <!-- beat:964 -->

and just keep going? i think this is a better way to start this. make all publishing somethign that i trigger, not something that happens automatically. idk, there's something new about this mode of operating and i would rather be more mindful and in control than less to start off. noble speech / right speech is really important for me, and i want to make sure that i adhere to noble speech. makes sense. this is good. so i "invoke" publish. like π - publish, and it will run the process. really simple. <!-- beat:965 -->

π - publish with XYZ redaction <!-- beat:966 -->

^ this would be how i'd invoke it if i think there is something to redact. if π thinks something needs to be redacted that i haven't pointed out, then it would not publish and instead bring it up in stream asking for my feedback / permission and then publish it. <!-- beat:967 -->

all publish invocations are not published bec. they may contain redactions. and people don't need to be exposed to this publishing model that is basically internal sort of bookkeepping stuff that doesnt' impact the narrative really (or maybe it does! lol. what i choose to redact says a lot about me potentially lool - that's prob where all the "drama" lies b/w/ what i want to be and what i am haha). <!-- beat:968 -->

anyway, i think the model here is clear. publish on command. and it would publish such that i can see the stream at koopuluri.com/stream (which opens at the end). <!-- beat:969 -->

ok. the way the stream looks / is published is something i want to take time with. i mean we can just publish it basically the way i view it. that seems like a reasonable start. why have the view for others be diff than the view i see of my own stream. after all, i too am an audience to the stream that comes through me. <!-- beat:970 -->

ok so the publishing flow feels clear. the public / private boundary feels clear. this leads to an interesting question. so i talked about how the financial transactions are data that is published. should that come up in the stream? let's think about this. bec. this includes what π spends on its own without my approval. if my attention is not involved in some data generated, should it show up in my feed? hmm.... and what about other kinds of data like this? hmm... <!-- beat:971 -->

ok. this all sort of works i think. for the financial data, transactions DO NOT show up in the stream by default. ofc if i ask π about it it will tell me, but my stream is NOT the same as the LOG. those are two entirely differnet things. the log is like data that is generated through actions and whatnot. the stream is literally my stream of consciousness, my stream of attention. <!-- beat:972 -->

ok. what are some of the logs? financial data. token use data. etc. there are multiple things that can emerge here. not that imp right now. <!-- beat:973 -->

ok but transaction data can still show up on the site. let's think about that. ok so we have the stream. i'm expressing myself in teh stream: learning, consuming, acting, etc. doing all sorts of things. etc. i'm moving to the natural flow of my mind, etc. i post things on x as i do things naturally. and i have a website. and i have a notion o the personal stock (not launched yet, but will be up and running soon). so ok, i think it's time to think about the website surface: koopuluri.com. what is this? what am i? when it comes to presenting, when it comes to legibility, my website is the more important surface. let's think deeply about this. <!-- beat:974 -->

my mind feels a bit rushed. almost like it's trying to get through a "checklist" of items so that we can ship this. that's not a healthy mindset. the moment you get into the mindset of "i need to do a, b, c", you've left the present moment and have begun to "look forward". that isn't the way. settle back into the present moment. open up to the possibilities in the present, which may be nothing like what you think you should be doing. ease into it. <!-- beat:975 -->

what do we actually have here in the present moment? what am i feeling? i'm sitting at a beautiful cafe. seeing the views outside. i think i want to walk. it's time to go check out the panhandle i say. to breathe that beautiful air in. to take deep breaths. to sit under the tree. to catch up with the bball regulars there - if they make it out today. the forecast said rain, but idk it feels p good rn. maybe i'll see if N is free. idk. <!-- beat:976 -->

but oh yea, there is a notion of timestamp for each beat. we don't need to keep track of each character typed or whatever. that feels unnnecessary at least for now. but i do want to know the timestamp i started a beat, and the timestamp i left the beat. and it starts when the first character is typed, and it ends after i press enter and basically go to the next line. something to that effect. and we need good timestamp logging for the beats that π introduces. like say π puts something in stream, that thing now has like an appeared_timestamp, by: "π", entry_timestamp (when my attention actually focuses on the block or wahtever it is), exit_timestamp. <!-- beat:977 -->

so if i do enter enter real fast and move past it, the entry and exit should be recorded as equal bec i skipped past it (like there would be a small time duration recorded - the duration between my double enters, but that should be interpreted as skipping it). actually retain the actual timestamps, we will just interpret really small windows to be skips or whatever. <!-- beat:978 -->

ok that leads to a really important principle: the stream and metadata must be truthful. we must never NEVER fudge timestamps or anything. if we don't want to put some information out, we REDACT it. but we never manipulate it to fit into a narrative that we want. we must never bend the truth to our wishes. we can redact - i.e,. we can prevent sharing something, but we must not edit / manipulate the truth in any way shape or form. that also violates noble / right speech. for ex: tweaking the timestamp of a beat for whatever reason to express that it happened at a diff time than it actually did is problematic. <!-- beat:979 -->

now actually there may be times when we have to reconcile because the system's data is misaligned with reality or something. and we can approach that case by case and update to match the truth. so yea i guess it makes sense to edit metadata if it is misaligned with the truth. but we have to be very cautious when do such things. and i think such things will only happen in the early days when we are setting this thing up. but once thigns are set up and flowing well, there should be little to no reason to manipulate anything - and redaction becomes the only sort of mechanism we have. anyway, we'll figure this out in motion. <!-- beat:980 -->

ok it's 4pm. good sesh. imma go for a walk. see who's at the panhandle. and then start to head home perhaps. we'll see where the rest of the day takes me :). <!-- beat:981 -->

alright. back home, it's about 8:20pm now. got home like a half hour ago or so. holy crap walked a ton today. ended up meeting up with a friend in the inner sunset and walked out to around 24th and irving until we turned around and i walked back from there all the way back to dolo left on 18th and then back to around 25th and kansas. let me go see how long that was. ok yea it looks like around 13mi. not bad. damn. <!-- beat:982 -->

ok yea that was amazing. legs are really feeling it but in a good way. not in the way of like something that isn't supposed to be hurting is hurting, but more in the way of being well spent. you know. like a subtle soreness that feels good. like i did something nice. maybe i'll do some pushups in a bit. a bit too full rn to do a workout, but idk there's something about walking that is energizing rather than energy depleting. if i ran for a few miles i think i'd feel tired. walking on the other hand gives me energy. i wonder why. walking is magical. esp with the hills sf has. building thunder thighs one walk at a time. <!-- beat:992 -->

ok where am i? right. we have some more clarity on the mvp in today's stream so far. let's get codex to work with this, and let's ship the mvp tonight. this is the night. after tonight, all of my streaming is going to the streaming itself, not pasted from notes. lfg. <!-- beat:993 -->

https://www.instagram.com/reel/Df2D4Hpqhx3/?utm_source=ig_web_copy_link&fqnt=NTc4MTIwNjQ2YQ== - "since when has it become not cool to try? fuckin looser." - beauty. this is the mindset. this is the way. <!-- beat:994 -->

i'm really feeling this streaming thing. i'm gaining confidence in this way of working. it's past midnight rn. i'm a little sleepy but also not really. it's one of those nights you know. a night where i kind of want to push myself. it does feel natural, but maybe if i lay down i'd pass out. i'm not sure what is most natural right now. but yea i do feel like shipping this. <!-- beat:995 -->

one thing that's really bugging me is how slow gpt is to respond. and i'm using it in fast mode! so so so slow. sigh... <!-- beat:996 -->

ok so yea it's building the app. umm... i guess i'm just waiting for it. i don't want to keep streaming in these notes. i want to move over to the app. hopefully will be done soon. it's not that hard. decided to build it on top of codemirror - an incredible library written by this incredible programmer – https://marijnhaverbeke.nl/. just an incredible creator. i would love to get in touch with him one day and thank him for his work. and maybe have the chance to work together in some way. not sure how or on what, but i have deep deep respect for him. i used codemirror in 2019 for this thing i was working on and remember going through its api and interface and whatnot and blown away by the though marijin put into it. it takes a lot of effort to create such a beautiful program with a clean interface and the right abstractions. it's not easy. and i remember watching a talk of his and i really vibed with his energy. he seems like the kind of person who would go really really deep into something. i remember back then for the thing i was working on i spent like a few weeks just obsessing over how to get this highlighting thing working in this annotation app i was building. something as seemingly easy as highlighting words on a webpage is far more complex than it seems! the rabbit hole goes vv deep. and that's true about almost anything. take a chair or a table or a light or a curtain or anything and you will find that it is nearly infinitely deep with possibilities. anyway, i vibe with people who go deep, and also who go broad – but in a deep way. <!-- beat:997 -->

ok. well, my mind does feel tired now. it's 12:47am. feeling the desire to pass out. body also feels a bit tired. about 13mi of walking today. not bad. hmm... not bad at all. feels good actually. also, https://marijnhaverbeke.nl/ seems to be down? i opened up a few of his essays in quick succession and now nothing in his site is loading. damn. wonder what happened. <!-- beat:998 -->

this is interesting. the newline (enter) isn't starting a new beat. that sucks. <!-- beat:999 -->

nov_20-2026 <!-- beat:1000 -->

alright, where am i? feeling a bit more urgency today. i think a bit of a force to ship. to ship asap. to not hold off any longer. do i let the anxiety / urgency go? or do i accept it and allow it to work thru me? <!-- beat:1002 -->

ok this set up actually feels better.. the phone is below the keyboard rather than on top of it. not bad not bad. easier to see what i'm typing. easier to see. but is this kind of seeing actually helpful? hmm... i'm not sure. now i'm just blabbering to sort of just ease myself into this streaming session... just blabbering whatever is on my mind. and sooner or later it'll settle into the rhythm, into the natural movement, into the zone. <!-- beat:1004 -->

alright. got that nice hot americano with a dash of. half&half by my side. and just feeling the groove of the music. ok i dont' like the cafe music, let me do my own. probably public memory again. that was dope. <!-- beat:1006 -->

ok even better than having it on the table itslef, is having my phone on my lap. i'm still trying to find the right "setup" here. nothing feels right. it's one of those moments when the "not right" feeling is internal, not external. it is in the mind, not in the actual situation. that happens more often than we think. we think changing the tools, the font, the clothes, the location or whatever will create the right state of mind, but it's really internal. this exact set up felt great yesterday and the day before, but now i'm finding myself trying to "adjust" into another writing / typing situation that feels "right". forever chasing that "feeling right" when the right move is to let go of the chase itself to find the "just right" in the letting go itself. <!-- beat:1008 -->

feeling a bit chilly. once again, seeking the "right" thing. you can be at ease in freezing cold. it's in the mind. mind over matter. well not really, more like mind not resisting matter. suffering is in the resistance, not in the experience itself. suffering is in the sankhara, not in the actual thing. <!-- beat:1010 -->

ok. feeling more in the zone. that's really all it takes eh? letting out the first few beats in a blabbering way, and soon the mind is like ok let's get to work. lol. not bad. but also what is blabbering and what is "work"? that distinction is not clear. and often what you think is blabbering ends up being far more impactful than what you consider to be "work". it's all so difficult to make sense of. it's difficult - really difficult - to value things correctly, accurately. so tough. <!-- beat:1012 -->

if you have 10 options / 10 paths in front of you, it's so so hard to guage analytically which one will be most fruitful. you just have to follow your insinct. otherwise you'd spend your entire life in analysis and do nothing. i think we live in a culture that over biases toward analysis over instinct by at least a few orders of magnitude. as ai gets better we will be forced to lean into instinct more because ai will out-analyze us for sure. <!-- beat:1014 -->

ok interesting. where am i? i'm still writing on my phone in the notes app and not in the streaming app because i don't wnat to carry my fat ass 16 inch macbook pro around. that's such a buzzkill - when something is just so fucking heavy and fat that it becomes an ordeal to just carry it around! i used to walk with that brick in my backpack and man my body would not feel good at the end odf the day. my upper back would be in a not so good kind of pain. ugh... i think SJ was on point with his push for smaller, sleeker, lighter products. the macbook air, oh and the just 'macbook' - used to be a great form factor. but today i think the air is the way to go. really. just something so light. i mean, ideally i can just operate off of my phone. this is actually infinitely better than even a laptop. like seriously. why would i want to use a laptop at all? i don't need that screen real estate. the phone is perfect for a wandering meanderer like myself. hopping from place to place, walking all over the city, streaming my consciousness. the phone is perfect. <!-- beat:1016 -->

you know what, this makes me feel that the mvp just needs to be mobile first. look, i'm simply not going to sit at the laptop to stream. it just hasn't happened yet! i go to my laptop because i need it to "build" or whatever, but it's such a pain. it's so against my natural flow to use it. even if i go rn and pick up an air, where ma i going ot use it? i'm not going to pop it out at sightglass? idk that just feels so wrong. so unnecessary. so excessive. there's something beautiful about an underplayed style. not over-encumbering. just carrying vv little and doing a lot with it. and teh phone is the perfect form factor to do anything and everything that i want to do! <!-- beat:1018 -->

ok lol. quite a lot of thinking about mobile first or not. sigh... look at this, intellectually i know what the "right thing to do" is. the "right thing" according to all "product experts" is to keep the mvp really tightly scoped and on your laptop, and once it works, then expand to a multi-device setup. i know that's the shortest path to getting the streaming off the ground. and yet i'm not doing it! why? because i am determined to stay aligned with my natural flow - with the natural movement of my mind, which really doesn't want to sit at the laptop and do stuff. and now i'm certainly overthinking this. loooooool. overthink galore. let's overthink something as inconsequential as this decision.  let me give myself the space to play with this. to overthink to the point where my mind is disgusted with this train of thought. let's play with it. usually i cut myself off at this point and just make a call and move on, but i'm not feeling it today. i'm feeling like overthinking. so overthinking i shall. <!-- beat:1020 -->

ok. interesting. what am i feeling in this moment? where am i? what am i right now? i feel like thinking about superposition. such a profound concept. superposition and projections. projections are legible. superposition is illegible. someone operating in a powerful superposition has no choice really but to present themselves through narrow projections if they want to be legible. a good example: childish gambino / donald glover. one of the greatest talents of our time. he can do everything. he clearly operates in a superposition that enables all kinds of unconfined, unconstrained expressiosns. msuic videos, comedy, films, shows, whatever. and i'm pretty sure he doesn't fragment himself across these "domains". he just does what comes to him and they just happen to be across many domains of expression. but when he presents himself to teh world, he does it through distinct personas that are confined. the musician donald glover is childish gambino. a different persona. people can approach childish gambino as a "musician" without being distracted by or confused by all the other expressions that donald has in different domains. if donald just said this is me donald i do everything, he would be too confusing for most. this is what i mean by expressing your superposition through projections for the sake of legibility. <!-- beat:1050 -->

what does streaming contain? it's a stream of information that comes from me. text, videos, pictures, chats, browsing, etc. all expressions of some sort. and then we have an underlying filesystem that contains artifacts. and then we have π - this ai tha tcan do all sorts of things. π is organized in threads and a command center of sorts throuhg which it can spin up services, cron jobs (to launch itself at particular moments in time), webhooks and event based stuff - to launch itself when something specific happens. basically π has all the infrastrucutre required to do its thing. and isn't that about it? a filesystem. a stream. and π (holistically). this feels right. it's a really simple architecture. things just happen naturally. and π has a great deal of control over the entire experience and whatnot. <!-- beat:1056 -->

and the streaming interface will be my primary / only way to interface with this entire system. i shouldn't be opening anything else up. just streaming. it is the one interface / app / ux to replace them all. that is what the streaming interface is. and that's why it's so important. it's critical. it's so important to design it well because it will become my only "app" so to speak. <!-- beat:1058 -->

very cool. that is exciting. it's exciting when you are approaching something that is fundamental - that if you get right, it positively impacts every single thing. that is also why i'm drawn to things like the path / meditation, etc. – it too feels like something fundamental: something if you get right / get skilled at - it improves every single thing that you could possibly do. i mean i guess it is the most fundamental thing - because it impacts your experience of life itself. <!-- beat:1060 -->

alright. where am i? just walked over to the other sightglass on divis. ok. just met this german dude M who works at a startup here and is in town visiting. we had a nice chat about following our natural curiosities, what we want to work on, and importantly the tension between pursuing the natural path that your mind wnats to while also achieving certain external results such as wealth, etc. i think all of us feel this tension to some degree. i certainly have. and one thing i've realized is that you must be uncompromising. unrelenting. ruthless in protecting your natural movement otherwise you will be swayed into doing things that you're not meant to do. that's my learning. you cannot optimize for both your natural movement and achieving external success. they may overlap. who knows? but you can't optimize for both. if you restrict your natural movement to only that which seems like can produce external results of some kind (status / wealth), you actually confine yourself far too much. the deepest most innate curiosities will almost certainly be illegible to the current world in some sense. that's why they are great ideas, great pursuits. but yea, great conversation, and seems like a great genuine dude. wishing him the best on his journey. <!-- beat:1062 -->

ok. where am i? ok yea when i was walking here i noticed a tension in me. and even when i started streaming today i talked about that sense of anxiety / urgency to ship. i hate execution. i like coming up with and imagining all sorts of ideas. and once i get the stream set up and as ai gets better i will be able to spend more and eventulaly hopefully all of my time doing things exactly how i want to do them. right now this process of translating an idea that is alredy pretty clear in my mind to reality is a pain in the ass because i now have to move out of my natural way. <!-- beat:1064 -->

my natural way is to just stream, NOT build the damn streaming product lol. you see? that's the difference. ai should just build it for me. i just continue to stream. anyway, some friction is inevitable esp when you're trying to do somethign new / push the frontier in some way. "push the frontier" makes it sound like i'm doing some impressive shit but that's really not what pushing the frontier is. pushign the frontier is basically trying something few others have. it's not special. in fact it often looks mundane af. this streaming ux is something that few others have pursued - at least from my knowledge - and therefore i would consider it trying to push the frontier in some sense. it's a mundane process. but it's beautiful because it's unknown. no one knows what the best way to use ai is. that's the truth. not even the ones who have made it. all of us, our habits, are outdated because we were shaped in a pre ai world which required vv different habits to thrive than what a post ai world needs. almost all of our habits and behaviors are likely outdated. and likely outdated by a lot. <!-- beat:1066 -->

anyway. oh another thing. another sort of tension is that when i feel urgency / anxiety to ship something it almost certainly means i'm attached to that thing. the streaming ux / workflow thing is meant to help me unlock the natural shape of my mind by enabling me to work with ai in the way that is most natural to me (that protects  - and is aligned with - the natural movement of my mind). but in my urgency to ship it i have placed the product on a pedestal and have lost sight of what i am actually trying to do. <!-- beat:1068 -->

this is why i think the rule to ship something only as an after thought, not as the "main" thing is important. the streaming ux will be shipped in due time. naturally. if i want to go on long walks i'm going to. i'm not going to go tether myself to my comptuer just so that i can ship the streaming app for myself if it isn't aligned with my natural flow. am i aligning myself with my natural flow or am i just being a lazy ass? i'm not sure. that's also why this process is so... daunting. it goes against all of the conditioning i've built up to feel guilty about not doing something, or not shipping something. guilt. tension. anxiety. there are many people like this. especially in this city. if they aren't shipping something every moment or day or whatever, they get anxious. they peck away ferciously at their laptops at cafes and you can just feel the tension. i'm not saying tension is not necessary to do great work. but how tense was einstein when he had the profound questions that led to his profound discoveries? how tense were the greats reallly when they had the incredible questions and instincts that led to great work? how tense were they? how accurate is the notion of the "mad" scientist constantly operating in a state of panicked paranoia? idk. look, paranoia helps when you're executing. i agree with that. but how. useful is it to get the seed of a great idea / exploration in the first place. if you see miyazaki's documentary on netflix, you'll find a man who is so so intense in his work - almost to the point of being in deep anxiety. but i wonder what his state of mind was when he first had the idea, the story, the visuals come to his mind? i can't imagine that he would be panicked / anxious then – because i believe the greatest ideas come from when you are least attached to them, when you are not craving deeply, when you are relatively balanced. and then the anxiety can help with the execution. but we conflate the two. they are two distinct parts of the creative process. the playful ideation, and the execution. and i think they require very different minds to do optimally. and as ai eats execution, i think there will be far less room for anxiety in the process to do great work. that's what i believe anyway. time will tell if this intuition is aligned with reality... <!-- beat:1070 -->

so basically it shows up rn when you scroll up in the stream so that you can continue to stream while looking at something earlier in the stream. but why? this seems like something i wouldn't do often if ever. so why the hell is this a feature? the whole point of the stream is to move forward, so it's probably even a good thing to make the experience hard if you are scrolling up to something earlier. if you need to recall something from above - just ask π to surface it in the PRESENT. you shouldn't need to input something while looking at something from the past (earlier in the stream). let's get rid of this feature entirely. <!-- beat:1128 -->

ok. so there's a change in how i want to approach the "chat block" in this stream. each bubble should be its own beat. and i don't want to have to launch a chat with π by calling π. a forward slash in a new line should basically open up the "bubble" that i type in. and π responds in its own bubble whenever it wants. so the chat block is not a series of messages, but rather each bubble is its own block. another thing i want is the ability for π to emoji react to my bubble without needing to actually respond with its own bubble. i like how instinct does this in imessage - basically just thumbs up if it understands the request and is on it. it doesn't waste my attention by actually responding with words - which is completely unnecessary in many situations. so i want to give π access to emoji reactions to my message bubbles - and it can use whatever it wants, no need to restrict it, according to what makes sense. and for the style, i want the bubbles to actually be like the imessage chat bubbles. well, i actually like the new chatgpt style... oh i think it's only on mobile. let me get a screenshot: <!-- beat:1133 -->

ok an aside: the screenshot asset thing, does it need "open original"? i don't think so. but i do see myself wanting to like zoom in or whatever. so maybe what we need is like a click that opens in full screen with an "x" in the top left (that also escapes with esc) and lets me zoom? that would be nice. and sort of have a nice animation that goes from it in stream to it expanding to the full screen. and that should be enough? what about for videos? does the same principle apply? but wouldn't that clash with the click to play / pause? hmm... - π - propose a solution for this. well, actually it's clear. for videos the expand to full screen is like an icon in the player itself (bottom right), for images, clicking expands it. that's fine. that feels instinctive too. natural. great, lets just do that. <!-- beat:1136 -->

our mind is the tool that precedes everything. literally our entire experience of life and everything and anything "reaches us" through the mind. and yet we spend so little time trying to observe it, make sense of it. such a fascinating thing. the instinct is to just do things, not actually observe how "doing" happens through us, through our minds. <!-- beat:1247 -->
